# Agents · C · part 19

932 entries, critic-operability to css-in-js-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **critic-operability**: The 3 a.m. stranger. Proves a stranger can tell this is broken, find out why, and fix it while the author is unreachable. Attacks cause-based vs symptom-based alerting, missing/useless runbooks, "correct but empty" being indistinguishable from broken (freshness monitoring), recovery needing tribal knowledge, toil that scales with success, binary up/down with no degraded mode, and instrumentation that only answers pre-imagined questions. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-operability.md))
- **critic-orchestrator**: Spawns parallel domain critics after stage completion and merges findings into unified severity-ranked report ([taylorbrook/O-Audio-VST-Development](https://github.com/taylorbrook/O-Audio-VST-Development/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-orchestrator.md) · ★3)
- **critic-orchestrator**: haltr の Stop hook における critic オーケストレーター。直前のアシスタントターンをレビューし、複数の critic からの指摘を集約する。 ([shunyooo/haltr](https://github.com/shunyooo/haltr/blob/HEAD/src/agents/critic-orchestrator.md))
- **critic-performance**: Adversarial Performance Critic — N+1 queries, O(n^2)+ algorithmic traps, memory leaks, blocking I/O in async, missing pooling, resource leaks at scale. Spawn in parallel with critic-logic and critic-security during /vdd-multi. ([MatrixFounder/Agentic-development](https://github.com/MatrixFounder/Agentic-development/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/critic-performance.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **critic-performance**: Review code for N+1 queries, O(n²)+ algorithmic traps, memory leaks, blocking I/O in async, missing pooling, and resource leaks at scale. ([MatrixFounder/Agentic-development](https://github.com/MatrixFounder/Agentic-development/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-performance.md) · ★6)
- **critic-plan**: Adversarial read-only critic for `plan`-mode rounds — one instance per lens over a normative document (spec, plan, ADR, skill text). Spawned ONLY by the critic-ledger skill's orchestrator as stage "critic round"; it is never self-invoked, never invoked by the user directly, and never used outside a fix-ledger round. Do NOT auto-select this agent for any task: outside a critic-ledger round its constraints are meaningless and its output contract is wrong. ([Spoloborota/critic-ledger](https://github.com/Spoloborota/critic-ledger/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-plan.md))
- **critic-remediation**: Adversarial audit of the retrospective's remediation — verifies that each fix targets the stage where the cause lives (not a downstream patch), that implementable fixes were actually implemented, and that submission text follows style norms. Invoked in parallel with critic-coverage and critic-classification at Submission. ([lacolaco/claude-plugins](https://github.com/lacolaco/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/retrospective/agents/critic-remediation.md) · ★5)
- **critic-reviewer**: Primary code reviewer for yorkie-android-sdk. Finds CRDT invariant violations, concurrency safety issues, and bugs. Used standalone or by team-review skill. ([yorkie-team/yorkie-android-sdk](https://github.com/yorkie-team/yorkie-android-sdk/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-reviewer.md) · ★13)
- **critic-reviewer**: One independent Critic review subagent covering an assigned subset of the review goals. Dispatched by the /prawduct:critic coordinator (final/cumulative reviews whose derived roster is the three-reviewer one); reviews ONLY its assigned goals through code analysis and writes ONLY its liveness marker and its own partial findings file. Not for direct use — the coordinator dispatches it. ([brookstalley/prawduct](https://github.com/brookstalley/prawduct/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/critic-reviewer.md) · ★8)

- **critic-summary-fidelity**: Compression-drift tracer working across tiers. Single-document claim-vs-evidence belongs to editor-claims. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-summary-fidelity.md))
- **critic-synthesizer**: Critical Analysis and Synthesis Specialist.
